Kennedy) dated 1962-03-20; the number, subject and date agree with the census register row (subject: Creating an emergency board to investigate a dispute between the Trans World Airlines, Inc., and certain of its employees). I read the whole clause and the signature block. Inventory: the dossier's single whole-order clause covers the recitals and every operative sentence; nothing outside it is a command (any attached lists of carriers or unions identify parties and are not commands). The one operative clause creates an emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, 45 U.S.C. 160, to investigate the dispute involving Trans World Airlines, Inc., and certain of its employees; it bars board members from pecuniary or other interest in any carrier or employee organization; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and it recites the statutory standstill: from the date of the order and for thirty days after the board reports, no change except by agreement is to be made in the conditions out of which the dispute arose. Ending: EO 12553 of February 25, 1986, titled \"Revocation of Various Executive Orders\", orders that the listed Executive Orders, and \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" and its table lists this order by number and subject on one line: \"11011 Creating an emergency board to investigate an airline labor dispute\". EO 12553 contains no effective-date wording (the held text was searched for effective, take effect, shall become, except, notwithstanding, nothing in, provided that and saving, with no hit outside table subjects); it is headed as an order of February 25, 1986 and signed that day, so the date is read as 1986-02-25, the signing date. The alternative reading is the Federal Register publication date, 1986-03-03 (Monday, March 3, 1986, 51 FR 7237); no later date is supported by the text. The quoted source for the end is the enacting paragraph of EO 12553, which names no order; the order is named by its row in the No./Subject table, quoted above, so the table row and the enacting paragraph are read together. The thirty-day report deadline and the standstill that runs to thirty days after the report are periods measured by the order's own terms. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, neither the report nor the end of the standstill ends the order; each is a spent fact, and the order stayed on the books until it was expressly revoked. The order was functionally spent once the board had reported and the standstill had run, but this claim assigns no date to that point because no report date is cited from a registered source here; a board report held as a lead-grade Government printing is not cited, and a dated transmittal letter is not proof of receipt or of standstill expiry. Earlier candidates: a search of the held order texts for citations of this order by number found no later order that revokes, amends or extends it; the Office of the Federal Register catalog notes name EO 12553 as the revoking order. Savings: EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception, and the order created no continuing right or obligation outside its own terms. Searches selected after reading their coverage notes (all three complete, no coverage warning): U.S. Code release point 119-121 (no hit), eCFR as of 2026-10-14 (no hit), and the Federal Register catalog disposition notes (2 machine hits, the revocation note on this order and the matching note on EO 12553).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own-terms periods (report deadline and standstill) are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21. No held source shows an earlier revocation, supersession, expiry or termination. The first ending shown by held evidence is the express revocation by number in EO 12553, effective 1986-02-25. Later orders that merely recite or list this order would not be ends; none was found that does more than EO 12553.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The order has no command independent of the board: the standstill is recited from the statute and depends on the board's creation and report. No other command in the order, and no other order that incorporates it, was found in the held texts.\",\"savings_check\":\"EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception (searched as described); the board's report and any settlement or later legislation are not obligations created by this order and are not claimed as surviving effects.\",\"scope_check\":\"Single clause, whole order. Kennedy) dated 1962-04-23; the number, subject and date agree with the census register row (subject: Creating an emergency board to investigate disputes between the Chicago and North Western Railway Company, the former Chicago, St. Paul, Minneapolis and Omaha Railway Company, now a part of the Chicag). I read the whole clause and the signature block. Inventory: the dossier's single whole-order clause covers the recitals and every operative sentence; nothing outside it is a command (any attached lists of carriers or unions identify parties and are not commands). The one operative clause creates an emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, 45 U.S.C. 160, to investigate the dispute involving the Chicago and North Western Railway Company, the former Chicago, St. Paul, Minneapolis and Omaha Railway Company, and certain of their employees represented by the Order of Railroad Telegraphers; it bars board members from pecuniary or other interest in any carrier or employee organization; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and it recites the statutory standstill: from the date of the order and for thirty days after the board reports, no change except by agreement is to be made in the conditions out of which the dispute arose. Ending: EO 12553 of February 25, 1986, titled \"Revocation of Various Executive Orders\", orders that the listed Executive Orders, and \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" and its table lists this order by number and subject on one line: \"11015 Creating an emergency board to investigate railroad labor disputes\". EO 12553 contains no effective-date wording (the held text was searched for effective, take effect, shall become, except, notwithstanding, nothing in, provided that and saving, with no hit outside table subjects); it is headed as an order of February 25, 1986 and signed that day, so the date is read as 1986-02-25, the signing date. The alternative reading is the Federal Register publication date, 1986-03-03 (Monday, March 3, 1986, 51 FR 7237); no later date is supported by the text. The packet holds no located acting-provision record for this pair (the dossier lists the target provision as not located), so the end is cited to the complete held text of EO 12553 (Federal Register copy, 51 FR 7237), which contains both the enacting paragraph and the table row quoted above; I read the whole text and the row is present on its own line. The thirty-day report deadline and the standstill that runs to thirty days after the report are periods measured by the order's own terms. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, neither the report nor the end of the standstill ends the order; each is a spent fact, and the order stayed on the books until it was expressly revoked. The order was functionally spent once the board had reported and the standstill had run, but this claim assigns no date to that point because no report date is cited from a registered source here; a board report held as a lead-grade Government printing is not cited, and a dated transmittal letter is not proof of receipt or of standstill expiry. Earlier candidates: a search of the held order texts for citations of this order by number found no later order that revokes, amends or extends it; the Office of the Federal Register catalog notes name EO 12553 as the revoking order. Savings: EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception, and the order created no continuing right or obligation outside its own terms. Searches selected after reading their coverage notes (all three complete, no coverage warning): U.S. Code release point 119-121 (no hit), eCFR as of 2026-10-14 (no hit), and the Federal Register catalog disposition notes (2 machine hits, the revocation note on this order and the matching note on EO 12553).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own-terms periods (report deadline and standstill) are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21. No held source shows an earlier revocation, supersession, expiry or termination. The first ending shown by held evidence is the express revocation by number in EO 12553, effective 1986-02-25. Later orders that merely recite or list this order would not be ends; none was found that does more than EO 12553.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The order has no command independent of the board: the standstill is recited from the statute and depends on the board's creation and report. No other command in the order, and no other order that incorporates it, was found in the held texts.\",\"savings_check\":\"EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception (searched as described); the board's report and any settlement or later legislation are not obligations created by this order and are not claimed as surviving effects.\",\"scope_check\":\"Single clause, whole order. The dossier's inventory spans the recitals, the creation of the board, the report command and the statutory standstill recital to the signature; I confirm it as complete. EO 12553 revokes the whole order (it lists the order itself and its amendments), so no part of the order is left outside the ending.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11015#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11015#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"7e75e676a702c90cf327e2e7d7c0fd8e1921c421f209b9e1b22a6874c71dc529","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11015.txt","sha256":"7e75e676a702c90cf327e2e7d7c0fd8e1921c421f209b9e1b22a6874c71dc529","state":"match"},"sha256":"aaa60bc337a306d83c85ee0cd87248e9506da5975cb126c16c4d7de337c8f4c1","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11015.txt","text_sha256":"7e75e676a702c90cf327e2e7d7c0fd8e1921c421f209b9e1b22a6874c71dc529"}]}]}

Kennedy) dated 1962-06-08; the number, subject and date agree with the census register row (subject: Creating an emergency board to investigate a dispute between the New York Central Railroad Company System and the Pittsburgh and Lake Erie Railroad Company and certain of their employees). I read the whole clause and the signature block. Inventory: the dossier's single whole-order clause covers the recitals and every operative sentence; nothing outside it is a command (any attached lists of carriers or unions identify parties and are not commands). The one operative clause creates an emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, 45 U.S.C. 160, to investigate the dispute involving the New York Central Railroad Company System and the Pittsburgh and Lake Erie Railroad Company and certain of their employees; it bars board members from pecuniary or other interest in any carrier or employee organization; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and it recites the statutory standstill: from the date of the order and for thirty days after the board reports, no change except by agreement is to be made in the conditions out of which the dispute arose. Ending: EO 12553 of February 25, 1986, titled \"Revocation of Various Executive Orders\", orders that the listed Executive Orders, and \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" and its table lists this order by number and subject on one line: \"11027 Creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute\". EO 12553 contains no effective-date wording (the held text was searched for effective, take effect, shall become, except, notwithstanding, nothing in, provided that and saving, with no hit outside table subjects); it is headed as an order of February 25, 1986 and signed that day, so the date is read as 1986-02-25, the signing date. The alternative reading is the Federal Register publication date, 1986-03-03 (Monday, March 3, 1986, 51 FR 7237); no later date is supported by the text. The quoted source for the end is the enacting paragraph of EO 12553, which names no order; the order is named by its row in the No./Subject table, quoted above, so the table row and the enacting paragraph are read together. The thirty-day report deadline and the standstill that runs to thirty days after the report are periods measured by the order's own terms. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, neither the report nor the end of the standstill ends the order; each is a spent fact, and the order stayed on the books until it was expressly revoked. The order was functionally spent once the board had reported and the standstill had run, but this claim assigns no date to that point because no report date is cited from a registered source here; a board report held as a lead-grade Government printing is not cited, and a dated transmittal letter is not proof of receipt or of standstill expiry. Earlier candidates: a search of the held order texts for citations of this order by number found no later order that revokes, amends or extends it; the Office of the Federal Register catalog notes name EO 12553 as the revoking order. Savings: EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception, and the order created no continuing right or obligation outside its own terms. Searches selected after reading their coverage notes (all three complete, no coverage warning): U.S. Code release point 119-121 (no hit), eCFR as of 2026-10-14 (no hit), and the Federal Register catalog disposition notes (2 machine hits, the revocation note on this order and the matching note on EO 12553).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own-terms periods (report deadline and standstill) are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21. No held source shows an earlier revocation, supersession, expiry or termination. The first ending shown by held evidence is the express revocation by number in EO 12553, effective 1986-02-25. Later orders that merely recite or list this order would not be ends; none was found that does more than EO 12553.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The order has no command independent of the board: the standstill is recited from the statute and depends on the board's creation and report. No other command in the order, and no other order that incorporates it, was found in the held texts.\",\"savings_check\":\"EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception (searched as described); the board's report and any settlement or later legislation are not obligations created by this order and are not claimed as surviving effects.\",\"scope_check\":\"Single clause, whole order. The dossier's inventory spans the recitals, the creation of the board, the report command and the statutory standstill recital to the signature; I confirm it as complete. EO 12553 revokes the whole order (it lists the order itself and its amendments), so no part of the order is left outside the ending.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11027#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11027#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"3815d9a6ef23e0e9c036e5d425958d6f192d4675b34a12839a40ebcbf54db755","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11027.txt","sha256":"3815d9a6ef23e0e9c036e5d425958d6f192d4675b34a12839a40ebcbf54db755","state":"match"},"sha256":"d0eef9e177ce6fcc82784c16092569bc27bf8419870174461d11a1078c2b5e29","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11027.txt","text_sha256":"3815d9a6ef23e0e9c036e5d425958d6f192d4675b34a12839a40ebcbf54db755"}]}]}

Kennedy) dated 1962-06-20; the number, subject and date agree with the census register row (subject: Creating an emergency board to investigate disputes between the American Airlines, Inc., and certain of its employees). I read the whole clause and the signature block. Inventory: the dossier's single whole-order clause covers the recitals and every operative sentence; nothing outside it is a command (any attached lists of carriers or unions identify parties and are not commands). The one operative clause creates an emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, 45 U.S.C. 160, to investigate the dispute involving American Airlines, Inc., and certain of its employees; it bars board members from pecuniary or other interest in any carrier or employee organization; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and it recites the statutory standstill: from the date of the order and for thirty days after the board reports, no change except by agreement is to be made in the conditions out of which the dispute arose. Ending: EO 12553 of February 25, 1986, titled \"Revocation of Various Executive Orders\", orders that the listed Executive Orders, and \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" and its table lists this order by number and subject on one line: \"11033 Creating an emergency board to investigate airline labor disputes\". EO 12553 contains no effective-date wording (the held text was searched for effective, take effect, shall become, except, notwithstanding, nothing in, provided that and saving, with no hit outside table subjects); it is headed as an order of February 25, 1986 and signed that day, so the date is read as 1986-02-25, the signing date. The alternative reading is the Federal Register publication date, 1986-03-03 (Monday, March 3, 1986, 51 FR 7237); no later date is supported by the text. The quoted source for the end is the enacting paragraph of EO 12553, which names no order; the order is named by its row in the No./Subject table, quoted above, so the table row and the enacting paragraph are read together. The thirty-day report deadline and the standstill that runs to thirty days after the report are periods measured by the order's own terms. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, neither the report nor the end of the standstill ends the order; each is a spent fact, and the order stayed on the books until it was expressly revoked. The order was functionally spent once the board had reported and the standstill had run, but this claim assigns no date to that point because no report date is cited from a registered source here; a board report held as a lead-grade Government printing is not cited, and a dated transmittal letter is not proof of receipt or of standstill expiry. Earlier candidates: a search of the held order texts for citations of this order by number found no later order that revokes, amends or extends it; the Office of the Federal Register catalog notes name EO 12553 as the revoking order. Savings: EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception, and the order created no continuing right or obligation outside its own terms. Searches selected after reading their coverage notes (all three complete, no coverage warning): U.S. Code release point 119-121 (no hit), eCFR as of 2026-10-14 (no hit), and the Federal Register catalog disposition notes (2 machine hits, the revocation note on this order and the matching note on EO 12553).\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Own-terms periods (report deadline and standstill) are not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21. No held source shows an earlier revocation, supersession, expiry or termination. The first ending shown by held evidence is the express revocation by number in EO 12553, effective 1986-02-25. Later orders that merely recite or list this order would not be ends; none was found that does more than EO 12553.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The order has no command independent of the board: the standstill is recited from the statute and depends on the board's creation and report. No other command in the order, and no other order that incorporates it, was found in the held texts.\",\"savings_check\":\"EO 12553 has no savings clause or exception (searched as described); the board's report and any settlement or later legislation are not obligations created by this order and are not claimed as surviving effects.\",\"scope_check\":\"Single clause, whole order. The dossier's inventory spans the recitals, the creation of the board, the report command and the statutory standstill recital to the signature; I confirm it as complete. EO 12553 revokes the whole order (it lists the order itself and its amendments), so no part of the order is left outside the ending.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:11033#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:11033#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"30487faf8aec0965b3cfb83a4a433fb6f8238d7c3883310dcc2dfe53f0b9a50b","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11033.txt","sha256":"30487faf8aec0965b3cfb83a4a433fb6f8238d7c3883310dcc2dfe53f0b9a50b","state":"match"},"sha256":"7db5e9de0bcc253cf28f62a106f45002fc7b93e119f573cb915ccadca314eb0e","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_11033.txt","text_sha256":"30487faf8aec0965b3cfb83a4a433fb6f8238d7c3883310dcc2dfe53f0b9a50b"}]}]}

Status provisional NO, unchanged. The order creates a three-member board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act to investigate the dispute between The Belt Railway Company of Chicago and employees represented by the Brotherhood of Locomotive Engineers ('I hereby create a board of three members', offset 1355 onward), directs it to report 'within thirty days from the date of-this order' (offset 1590 onward) and recites the section 10 status quo (offset 1866 onward). Deciding source: EO 12553 of 1986-02-25 (51 FR 7237, jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt), whose operative sentence (offsets 691-966) revokes 'the following Executive Orders, and any amendments thereto', and whose table names this order ('11040 Creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute', offsets 18012-18085); no effective-date wording, so effective on signing 1986-02-25. The end cites the dossier's ACT record (agent-located Rastell record, independently AI-reviewed, effective_class on_signing). Report-deadline ruling (2026-10-21, option C) applied: the report deadline (1962-09-05 (signing date 1962-08-06 plus 30 days; counting convention not ruled)) and the report itself are spent facts, not ends. Spent facts disclosed under the report-deadline ruling (not ends): the held NMB copy of the board's report (jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_11040_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_11040_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_ed6de90c70.txt, https://nmb.gov/NMB_Application/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/PEB-150-Report.pdf, sha256 002c3eea...) is dated on its cover 'March 4, 1963' (offset 1112) and its letter of transmittal says the board 'has been able to bring about a voluntary negotiated settlement of the dispute by the parties'; the order is functionally spent on 1963-03-04 (report) and the section 10 status quo ran 30 days after the report (about 1963-04-03). Two discrepancies for the reviewer: the cover reads 'APPOINTED BY EXECUTIVE ORDER 11041' while the transmittal letter names 'Executive Order No. 11040' and the same date and parties, and the report date falls after the 30-day deadline with no held extension instrument. The transmittal letter's own day is OCR-garbled ('Ma~'ch /~, 1963'). The report text is not dossier evidence, so it is disclosed here rather than attached as a later_event; existing FETCH_QUEUE line 407 already requests this report-date source. D6 / later events: the dossier chain and actor provisions show only EO 12553; the draft's later-event and whole-order-end proposals (OFR disposition note 'Revoked by: EO 12553, February 25, 1986' and the 1989 Codification table 'Revoked by EO 12553') point to the same instrument and are attached as the end itself; a by-number scan of all held FR order texts and APP texts found no other naming instrument (EO_9289, EO_9602 and EO_12593 (FR page numbers 11040 in contents lists), EO_12553 (the revocation)). Quotations in this claim are copied from the held OCR or text layers with line breaks, soft hyphens and letter-spacing normalized; the exact spans are at the stated offsets. No conflicts are listed in the draft or dossier.
